/* 不思議の塔: 筆算（マス目に書く形）
 * 「式は合っているのに 計算だけまちがえた」ときに、筆算をマス目の中に1ステップずつ書いていく。
 * 終わると、書き終わった筆算がそのまま画面に残る（割り算の筆算アプリのやり直し画面と同じ形）。
 *   mul: かけ算の筆算。かける数が1けたなら 位ごとに（くり上がりつき）、2けた以上なら 段ごとに書いてたす
 *   div: わり算の筆算。たてる → かける → ひく（→ おろす）
 *   add: たし算（分けた長方形の面積を たす）
 * grid: 描くための行。cells は 右づめ。stepKey のある行は そのステップが終わると数字が入る
 */
(function (root, factory) {
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = factory();
  else { root.MT = root.MT || {}; root.MT.Hissan = factory(); }
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this, function () {
  'use strict';

  const PLACE = ['一の位', '十の位', '百の位', '千の位', '万の位'];

  function digitsOf(n) {
    return String(Math.abs(Math.trunc(n))).split('').map(Number).reverse(); // 一の位から
  }

  function rightCells(cols, text) {
    // 右づめで cells に入れる
    const cells = new Array(cols).fill('');
    const t = String(text);
    for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i += 1) cells[cols - t.length + i] = t.charAt(i);
    return cells;
  }

  function shiftedCells(cols, text, shift) {
    const cells = new Array(cols).fill('');
    const t = String(text);
    for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i += 1) {
      const idx = cols - shift - t.length + i;
      if (idx >= 0 && idx < cols) cells[idx] = t.charAt(i);
    }
    return cells;
  }

  // ---- かけ算 ----
  function planMul(a0, b0) {
    let a = a0, b = b0;
    if (String(b).length > String(a).length) { a = b0; b = a0; }   // 上に大きい数
    const product = a * b;
    const cols = String(product).length + 1;   // 左に「×」の分
    const steps = [];
    const grid = [];
    grid.push({ type: 'operand', cells: rightCells(cols, a) });
    grid.push({ type: 'operand', cells: rightCells(cols, b), sign: '×' });
    grid.push({ type: 'divider' });
    const bd = digitsOf(b);
    if (bd.length === 1) {
      // 位ごとに: 一の位から (a の各位 × b + くり上がり)
      const ad = digitsOf(a);
      let carry = 0;
      const rowSteps = [];
      ad.forEach(function (d, i) {
        const v = d * b + carry;
        const last = i === ad.length - 1;
        const write = last ? v : v % 10;
        const next = last ? 0 : Math.floor(v / 10);
        const expr = d + '×' + b + (carry ? '＋' + carry : '');
        steps.push({ key: 'd' + i, label: PLACE[i] || '', expr: expr, answer: v, write: String(write), carry: next, place: i, hint: carry ? 'くり上がった ' + carry + ' を たすよ' : null });
        rowSteps.push({ key: 'd' + i, place: i, write: String(write), carry: next });
        carry = next;
      });
      grid.push({ type: 'product', digitSteps: rowSteps, cols: cols });
    } else {
      const partials = [];
      bd.forEach(function (d, j) {
        if (d === 0) return;
        const v = a * d;
        steps.push({ key: 'p' + j, label: (PLACE[j] || '') + 'の だん', expr: a + '×' + d, answer: v, shift: j, hint: j > 0 ? 'この だんは ' + (j === 1 ? '十' : j === 2 ? '百' : '') + 'の位から 書き始めるよ' : null });
        partials.push({ key: 'p' + j, value: v, shift: j });
        grid.push({ type: 'partial', stepKey: 'p' + j, cells: shiftedCells(cols, v, j), shift: j });
      });
      if (partials.length > 1) {
        grid.push({ type: 'divider' });
        const sumExpr = partials.map(function (p) { return p.value * Math.pow(10, p.shift); }).join('＋');
        steps.push({ key: 'sum', label: 'たす', expr: sumExpr, answer: product, isSum: true });
        grid.push({ type: 'sum', stepKey: 'sum', cells: rightCells(cols, product) });
      } else if (partials.length === 1) {
        // だんが1つなら、それが答え
        grid[grid.length - 1].isAnswer = true;
      }
    }
    return { op: 'mul', a: a, b: b, cols: cols, title: a + '×' + b + ' の筆算', steps: steps, grid: grid, answer: product, index: 0, fails: 0 };
  }

  // ---- わり算（割り算の筆算アプリと同じ組み立て） ----
  function planDiv(a, b) {
    const dividendDigits = String(a).split('').map(Number);
    const cols = dividendDigits.length;
    const steps = [];
    const rows = [];       // grid の本体（product / carry）
    const quotientCells = new Array(cols).fill('');
    let carry = 0;
    let started = false;
    for (let index = 0; index < dividendDigits.length; index += 1) {
      const digit = dividendDigits[index];
      const partial = carry * 10 + digit;
      if (!started && partial < b) { carry = partial; continue; }
      started = true;
      const qd = Math.floor(partial / b);
      const productVal = qd * b;
      const rest = partial - productVal;
      steps.push({ key: 'q' + index, label: 'たてる', expr: partial + '÷' + b, answer: qd, kind: 'quotient', index: index, hint: b + 'に 何をかけると ' + partial + ' に 一番近くなる?' });
      quotientCells[index] = { stepKey: 'q' + index, text: String(qd) };
      if (qd > 0) {
        steps.push({ key: 'p' + index, label: 'かける', expr: b + '×' + qd, answer: productVal, kind: 'product', index: index });
        rows.push({ type: 'product', stepKey: 'p' + index, text: String(productVal), start: index - String(productVal).length + 1, end: index, underline: true });
        const hasNext = index < dividendDigits.length - 1;
        const shown = hasNext ? String(rest) + dividendDigits[index + 1] : String(rest);
        const nextChunk = hasNext ? Number(shown) : rest;
        steps.push({ key: 'r' + index, label: hasNext ? 'ひいて、おろす' : 'ひく', expr: partial + '−' + productVal, answer: rest, kind: 'sub', index: index, hint: hasNext ? 'ひいたら、次の位の ' + dividendDigits[index + 1] + ' を おろすよ' : (rest === 0 ? 'ぴったり わり切れる' : 'あまりが 出る') });
        const endIdx = hasNext ? index + 1 : index;
        rows.push({ type: 'carry', stepKey: 'r' + index, text: shown, start: endIdx - shown.length + 1, end: endIdx, remainder: !hasNext && rest > 0 });
        carry = rest;   // 次の位を おろすのは 次のくり返しで（partial = carry×10 + 次の数字）
        continue;
      }
      carry = partial;
    }
    if (!started) quotientCells[cols - 1] = { stepKey: null, text: '0' };
    const q = Math.floor(a / b), r = a % b;
    return {
      op: 'div', a: a, b: b, cols: cols, title: a + '÷' + b + ' の筆算',
      steps: steps,
      grid: { divisor: String(b), quotientCells: quotientCells, dividendCells: dividendDigits.map(String), rows: rows },
      answer: q, remainder: r, index: 0, fails: 0
    };
  }

  // ---- たし算（分けた長方形の面積を たす） ----
  function planAdd(parts) {
    const total = parts.reduce(function (s, v) { return s + v; }, 0);
    const cols = String(total).length + 1;
    const steps = [];
    const grid = [];
    parts.forEach(function (p, i) { grid.push({ type: 'operand', cells: rightCells(cols, p), sign: i === parts.length - 1 ? '＋' : '' }); });
    grid.push({ type: 'divider' });
    let acc = parts[0];
    for (let i = 1; i < parts.length; i += 1) {
      steps.push({ key: 's' + i, label: 'たす', expr: acc + '＋' + parts[i], answer: acc + parts[i] });
      acc += parts[i];
    }
    if (!steps.length) steps.push({ key: 's0', label: 'そのまま', expr: String(acc), answer: acc });
    grid.push({ type: 'sum', stepKey: steps[steps.length - 1].key, cells: rightCells(cols, total) });
    return { op: 'add', parts: parts.slice(), cols: cols, title: parts.join('＋') + ' の計算', steps: steps, grid: grid, answer: total, index: 0, fails: 0 };
  }

  function plan(op, a, b) {
    if (op === 'mul') return planMul(a, b);
    if (op === 'div') return planDiv(a, b);
    if (op === 'add') return planAdd(Array.isArray(a) ? a : [a, b]);
    throw new Error('unknown op ' + op);
  }

  function current(h) {
    return h && h.index < h.steps.length ? h.steps[h.index] : null;
  }

  function stepIndexOf(h, key) {
    for (let i = 0; i < h.steps.length; i += 1) if (h.steps[i].key === key) return i;
    return -1;
  }

  function stepState(h, key) {
    // 'done' | 'active' | 'todo'
    const i = stepIndexOf(h, key);
    if (i < 0) return 'todo';
    if (i < h.index) return 'done';
    if (i === h.index) return 'active';
    return 'todo';
  }

  function answer(h, value) {
    const step = current(h);
    if (!step) return { correct: false, done: true };
    const n = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isInteger(n)) return { correct: false, empty: true };
    if (n !== step.answer) { h.fails += 1; return { correct: false, step: step, fails: h.fails }; }
    h.index += 1;
    h.fails = 0;
    return { correct: true, done: h.index >= h.steps.length, next: current(h), result: h.answer };
  }

  function isDone(h) { return !!h && h.index >= h.steps.length; }

  return { plan: plan, current: current, answer: answer, stepState: stepState, stepIndexOf: stepIndexOf, isDone: isDone, PLACE: PLACE, digitsOf: digitsOf };
});


/* 不思議の塔: 複合図形のガイド（補助線 → 分けた長方形をひとつずつ → たす）
 * mode: 'full'  たて・よこ・面積を1つずつ聞く（12階、やり直し）
 *       'half'  「どこで切る?」のあと、面積のらんだけ（13階）
 *       'free'  線は引いても引かなくてもよい。答えのらんだけ（14階）
 *       'three' たて・横・囲んでひく の3通りで解く（12階の関所）
 *       'cutonly' 長方形に 分けられたら おわり（12階の 1問目。たて・よこ・かこむ の どれでも よい）
 */
(function (root, factory) {
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = factory(require('./util.js'), require('./figure.js'), require('./deduce.js'));
  else { root.MT = root.MT || {}; root.MT.Guide = factory(root.MT.Util, root.MT.Figure, root.MT.Deduce); }
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this, function (Util, Figure, Deduce) {
  'use strict';

  function create(q, mode) {
    const scale = (q.cut && q.cut.scale) || 1;
    return {
      mode: mode || (q.cut && q.cut.guide) || 'full',
      scale: scale,
      unit: (q.figure && q.figure.unit) || 'cm',
      lines: [],
      analysis: null,
      way: null,            // 'v' | 'h' | 'e'
      steps: [],
      stepIndex: 0,
      waysDone: [],
      hints: 0,
      stops: {},
      finished: false,
      message: ''
    };
  }

  function wayOf(analysis, lines) {
    if (analysis.mode === 'enclose') return 'e';
    const vertical = lines.every(function (l) { return l.x1 === l.x2; });
    const horizontal = lines.every(function (l) { return l.y1 === l.y2; });
    if (vertical) return 'v';
    if (horizontal) return 'h';
    return 'x';
  }

  function dimText(v, unit) { return v + unit; }

  function partSteps(state, q, parts, total, opts) {
    const s = state.scale;
    const u = state.unit;
    const u2 = u + '²';
    const steps = [];
    const mode = state.mode === 'three' ? 'full' : state.mode;
    parts.forEach(function (p, i) {
      const name = (opts && opts.names && opts.names[i]) || (Figure.describeRectPosition(p, q.figure.fig) + 'の長方形');
      if (mode === 'full') {
        // at: その長方形の 左の辺・下の辺の 場所（解説で 赤い 太線を 引くため。本人の指定 2026-09-16）
        steps.push({ key: 'p' + i + '-h', part: i, ask: name + 'の たては?', answer: p.h * s, unit: u, dim: { axis: 'y', a: p.y, b: p.y + p.h, at: p.x } });
        steps.push({ key: 'p' + i + '-w', part: i, ask: name + 'の 横は?', answer: p.w * s, unit: u, dim: { axis: 'x', a: p.x, b: p.x + p.w, at: p.y } });
      }
      steps.push({ key: 'p' + i + '-area', part: i, ask: name + 'の 面積は?', answer: p.w * p.h * s * s, unit: u2, formula: (p.h * s) + '×' + (p.w * s), calc: ['mul', p.h * s, p.w * s] });
    });
    if (opts && opts.enclose) {
      steps.push({ key: 'sub', ask: '大きい長方形から 欠けたところをひくと?', answer: total * s * s, unit: u2, formula: parts.map(function (p, i) { return (i === 0 ? '' : '−') + (p.w * p.h * s * s); }).join('') });
    } else {
      steps.push({ key: 'sum', ask: '全部たすと?', answer: total * s * s, unit: u2, formula: parts.map(function (p) { return p.w * p.h * s * s; }).join('+'), calc: ['add', parts.map(function (p) { return p.w * p.h * s * s; })] });
    }
    return steps;
  }

  function addLine(state, q, line) {
    if (state.finished) return { ok: false, message: 'もう切り終わっているよ。' };
    const fig = q.figure.fig;
    const trial = state.lines.concat([line]);
    const analysis = Figure.analyzeLines(fig, trial);
    if (analysis.mode === 'invalid') {
      const msg = analysis.reason === 'not-axis' ? '線は たてか 横に まっすぐ引こう。' : analysis.reason === 'outside' ? '図形の外の線は、かこむ長方形のふちに引こう。' : 'そこでは 切れないよ。';
      return { ok: false, message: msg, analysis: analysis };
    }
    state.lines = trial;
    state.analysis = analysis;
    if (analysis.mode === 'partial') {
      state.message = analysis.reason === 'not-separated' ? 'まだ分かれていないよ。図形を通るように引こう。' : 'まだ長方形になっていないところがある。もう1本引こう。';
      return { ok: true, pending: true, message: state.message, analysis: analysis };
    }
    const way = wayOf(analysis, trial);
    if (state.mode === 'three') {
      const wanted = (q.cut && q.cut.modes) || ['v', 'h', 'e'];
      if (way === 'x' || wanted.indexOf(way) < 0) {
        state.lines = []; state.analysis = null;
        return { ok: false, message: 'たてだけ、横だけ、または かこんでひく で切ろう。', analysis: analysis };
      }
      if (state.waysDone.indexOf(way) >= 0) {
        state.lines = []; state.analysis = null;
        return { ok: false, message: 'その切り方は もうやったよ。別の切り方で。', analysis: analysis };
      }
    }
    state.way = way;
    if (state.mode === 'cutonly') {
      state.steps = []; state.stepIndex = 0; state.message = '';
      state.waysDone.push(way);
      state.finished = true;
      return { ok: true, pending: false, finished: true, analysis: analysis, way: way, steps: [] };
    }
    if (analysis.mode === 'enclose') {
      const parts = [analysis.box].concat(analysis.cutouts);
      const names = ['かこむ大きい長方形'].concat(analysis.cutouts.map(function () { return '欠けたところ'; }));
      state.steps = partSteps(state, q, parts, analysis.total, { enclose: true, names: names });
    } else {
      state.steps = partSteps(state, q, analysis.parts, analysis.total, {});
    }
    if (state.mode === 'free') state.steps = [];
    state.stepIndex = 0;
    state.message = '';
    return { ok: true, pending: false, analysis: analysis, way: way, steps: state.steps };
  }

  function clearLines(state) {
    state.lines = [];
    state.analysis = null;
    state.steps = [];
    state.stepIndex = 0;
    state.way = null;
    state.message = '';
  }

  function currentStep(state) {
    if (!state.steps.length || state.stepIndex >= state.steps.length) return null;
    return state.steps[state.stepIndex];
  }

  function answerStep(state, q, value) {
    const step = currentStep(state);
    if (!step) return { correct: false, message: 'まず線を引こう。' };
    const n = Util.parseInteger(value);
    if (n == null) return { correct: false, empty: true, message: '数を入れてね。' };
    if (n !== step.answer) {
      state.stops[step.key] = (state.stops[step.key] || 0) + 1;
      const out = { correct: false, step: step, stopCount: state.stops[step.key] };
      // 分けた長方形の面積・たし算・ひき算 のステップは「式は決まっている」ので、計算の筆算を出せる
      if (step.calc) out.calc = step.calc;
      return out;
    }
    state.stepIndex += 1;
    const doneWay = state.stepIndex >= state.steps.length;
    if (doneWay) {
      state.waysDone.push(state.way);
      if (state.mode === 'three') {
        const wanted = (q.cut && q.cut.modes) || ['v', 'h', 'e'];
        const remaining = wanted.filter(function (w) { return state.waysDone.indexOf(w) < 0; });
        if (remaining.length) {
          state.lines = []; state.analysis = null; state.steps = []; state.stepIndex = 0; state.way = null;
          return { correct: true, wayDone: true, remaining: remaining, message: '次は ' + wayName(remaining[0]) + ' で。' };
        }
        state.finished = true;
        return { correct: true, wayDone: true, allWaysDone: true, finalChoice: q.finalChoice };
      }
      state.finished = true;
      return { correct: true, wayDone: true, finished: true, total: step.answer };
    }
    return { correct: true, next: currentStep(state) };
  }

  function wayName(way) {
    return { v: 'たてに切る', h: '横に切る', e: 'かこんでひく' }[way] || way;
  }

  function hint(state, q) {
    // 次の一歩だけ出す。使った回数を記録する
    state.hints += 1;
    const step = currentStep(state);
    if (!state.analysis) return { text: 'まず、図形が長方形に分かれるところに 線を引こう。角と角を 結ぶよ。', step: null };
    if (!step) return { text: '答えのらんに 面積を入れよう。', step: null };
    if (step.dim) {
      const hiddenHit = (q.figure.hidden || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === step.dim.axis && l.a === step.dim.a && l.b === step.dim.b; })[0];
      if (hiddenHit) {
        const sol = Deduce.solve(q.figure.fig, q.figure.labels);
        const gaps = sol.gaps[step.dim.axis].filter(function (g) { return g.a >= step.dim.a && g.b <= step.dim.b; });
        const exprs = gaps.filter(function (g) { return g.path && g.path.length > 1; }).map(function (g) { return g.expr + '＝' + g.value; });
        return { text: 'この辺は 書いていないね。向かい合う辺は同じ長さ。' + (exprs.length ? exprs.join('、') + ' で出せるよ。' : '書いてある辺から 出せるよ。'), step: step };
      }
      const shown = (q.figure.labels || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === step.dim.axis && l.a === step.dim.a && l.b === step.dim.b; })[0];
      const labelText = shown && shown.text != null ? shown.text : dimText(step.answer, step.unit);
      return { text: '図に書いてある ' + labelText + ' の辺を見よう。', step: step };
    }
    if (step.formula) return { text: '式は ' + step.formula + ' だよ。', step: step };
    return { text: 'もう一度 図を見てみよう。', step: step };
  }

  // 正しい切り方を自動で見つける（やり直し画面で、最初から切った図を見せるため）
  // way: 'v' たてだけ | 'h' よこだけ | 'e' 囲んでひく | null（分けた数が少ないほう。同じなら たて）
  function autoLines(fig, way) {
    const coords = Figure.coordinates(fig);
    const b = Figure.bounds(fig);
    const vLine = function (x) { return { x1: x, y1: b.y1, x2: x, y2: b.y2 }; };
    const hLine = function (y) { return { x1: b.x1, y1: y, x2: b.x2, y2: y }; };
    if (way === 'e') {
      const cells = Figure.cellSet(fig);
      for (let x = b.x1; x < b.x2; x += 1) {
        for (let y = b.y1; y < b.y2; y += 1) {
          if (cells.has(Figure.key(x, y))) continue;
          if (y === b.y2 - 1) return [{ x1: x, y1: b.y2, x2: x + 1, y2: b.y2 }];
          if (y === b.y1) return [{ x1: x, y1: b.y1, x2: x + 1, y2: b.y1 }];
          if (x === b.x2 - 1) return [{ x1: b.x2, y1: y, x2: b.x2, y2: y + 1 }];
          if (x === b.x1) return [{ x1: b.x1, y1: y, x2: b.x1, y2: y + 1 }];
        }
      }
      return null;
    }
    const tryWay = function (w) {
      const candidates = [];
      if (w !== 'h') coords.xs.slice(1, -1).forEach(function (x) { candidates.push(vLine(x)); });
      if (w !== 'v') coords.ys.slice(1, -1).forEach(function (y) { candidates.push(hLine(y)); });
      let lines = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
        const trial = lines.concat([candidates[i]]);
        const a = Figure.analyzeLines(fig, trial);
        if (a.mode === 'cut') return { lines: trial, parts: a.parts.length };
        if (a.mode === 'partial' && a.reason === 'part-not-rect') lines = trial;
      }
      return null;
    };
    if (way) { const r = tryWay(way); return r ? r.lines : null; }
    const v = tryWay('v'), h = tryWay('h');
    if (v && h) return h.parts < v.parts ? h.lines : v.lines;
    const one = v || h;
    return one ? one.lines : null;
  }

  function presetCut(state, q, way) {
    // 線を自動で引いて、ステップ（たて・よこ・面積・たす）を用意する。引けなければ null（子どもが引く）
    const lines = autoLines(q.figure.fig, way || null);
    if (!lines) return null;
    let res = null;
    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
      res = addLine(state, q, lines[i]);
      if (!res.ok) { clearLines(state); return null; }
    }
    if (res && res.pending) { clearLines(state); return null; }
    state.preset = true;
    return res;
  }

  function partsForDisplay(state) {
    if (!state.analysis) return [];
    if (state.analysis.mode === 'enclose') return [Object.assign({ role: 'box' }, state.analysis.box)].concat(state.analysis.cutouts.map(function (c) { return Object.assign({ role: 'cutout' }, c); }));
    if (state.analysis.mode === 'cut') return state.analysis.parts.map(function (p) { return Object.assign({ role: 'part' }, p); });
    return (state.analysis.parts || []).map(function (p) { return Object.assign({ role: 'part' }, p); });
  }

  return {
    create: create,
    addLine: addLine,
    clearLines: clearLines,
    currentStep: currentStep,
    answerStep: answerStep,
    hint: hint,
    wayName: wayName,
    partsForDisplay: partsForDisplay,
    autoLines: autoLines,
    presetCut: presetCut
  };
});


/* 不思議の塔: 1つの階の10問を組み立てて、答えを記録する
 * 混ぜ方: その階の問題（教える順）＋ 前の階で間違えた問題を数字を変えて1〜2問 ＋ 下の階の関所を抜き打ちで1〜2問
 * 同じ形式を3問続けない（できる範囲で並べかえる）
 */
(function (root, factory) {
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = factory(require('./util.js'), require('./bank.js'), require('./floors.js'));
  else { root.MT = root.MT || {}; root.MT.Session = factory(root.MT.Util, root.MT.Bank, root.MT.Floors); }
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this, function (Util, Bank, Floors) {
  'use strict';

  function formatClass(templateId, params) {
    return Bank.get(templateId).format;
  }

  // 「同じ形式を3問続けない」の形式 = 問い方。答え方の部品が同じでも、問い方がちがえば別の形式
  const KIND = {
    'f2-compare': 'compare', 'f3-larger': 'compare', 'f3-gate-sameperim': 'compare', 'f3-transpose': 'compare',
    'f7-cmp-area-sameperim': 'compare', 'f7-cmp-perim-samearea': 'compare',
    'f3-peer-perim': 'peer', 'f7-gate-peer': 'peer', 'f7-peer-right': 'peer', 'f8-gate-peer100': 'peer', 'f10-gate-peer-ground': 'peer', 'f14-peer': 'peer',
    'f3-gate-what12': 'meaning', 'f4-gate-why': 'meaning', 'f5-gate-what3': 'meaning', 'f8-gate-why10000': 'meaning', 'f12-gate-why-not': 'meaning', 'f13-gate-why-minus': 'meaning', 'f14-gate-enclose-expr': 'meaning',
    'f3-square': 'square', 'f5-square': 'square', 'f6-square-from-area': 'square',
    'f5-diagonal': 'trap', 'f5-perim-extra': 'trap',
    'f3-reverse-rows': 'reverse', 'f4-reverse': 'reverse', 'f4-missing': 'reverse', 'f6-reverse': 'reverse', 'f6-missing': 'reverse',
    'f6-gate-possible': 'possible',
    'f8-m2cm2': 'convert', 'f8-cm2m2': 'convert', 'f9-ladder-conv': 'convert', 'f9-ladder-fill': 'ladder',
    'f8-rect-m-cm2': 'unit-rect', 'f8-mixed': 'unit-rect',
    'f9-unit-square': 'unit-def', 'f9-gate-a-shape': 'unit-def', 'f9-gate-x10': 'scale',
    'f10-magnitude': 'magnitude', 'f10-desk': 'magnitude', 'f10-approx': 'approx',
    'f11-same-area': 'same-area', 'f11-compare': 'compare', 'f11-order': 'order', 'f11-scene-convert': 'convert',
    'f12-side-first': 'side', 'f13-half-t': 'cut-t', 'f14-mixed-unit': 'cut-unit', 'f14-both': 'fields',
    'f4-word': 'word', 'f6-word-reverse': 'word', 'f9-field-a': 'word',
    'f7-max-area': 'compare', 'f14-road': 'road', 'f14-frame': 'frame'
  };

  function kindOf(templateId) {
    return KIND[templateId] || Bank.get(templateId).format;
  }

  function maxRun(items) {
    let run = 1, best = 1;
    for (let i = 1; i < items.length; i += 1) {
      run = items[i].kind === items[i - 1].kind ? run + 1 : 1;
      best = Math.max(best, run);
    }
    return best;
  }

  function lowerGateTemplates(floorNo) {
    const out = [];
    Floors.FLOORS.forEach(function (f) {
      if (f.no >= floorNo || f.no < 2) return;
      f.own.forEach(function (item) {
        if (Bank.get(item.t).gate) out.push({ t: item.t, p: item.p, floor: f.no });
      });
    });
    return out;
  }

  function fixFormatRuns(items) {
    // 同じ問い方が3つ続くときだけ、一番多い問い方を「それ以外」で均等に区切る。
    // 続いていなければ 教える順のまま。例: 数7・選択2・ペア1 → 数 数 選 数 数 ペ 数 数 選 数
    if (items.length <= 2) return items.slice();
    if (maxRun(items) <= 2) return items.slice();
    const counts = {};
    items.forEach(function (it) { counts[it.kind] = (counts[it.kind] || 0) + 1; });
    let major = null;
    Object.keys(counts).forEach(function (f) { if (major == null || counts[f] > counts[major]) major = f; });
    const majors = items.filter(function (it) { return it.kind === major; });
    const others = items.filter(function (it) { return it.kind !== major; });
    if (!others.length) return items.slice();
    const separators = fixFormatRuns(others);
    const groups = separators.length + 1;
    const base = Math.floor(majors.length / groups);
    const extra = majors.length % groups;
    const out = [];
    let cursor = 0;
    for (let g = 0; g < groups; g += 1) {
      const size = base + (g < extra ? 1 : 0);
      for (let i = 0; i < size; i += 1) out.push(majors[cursor + i]);
      cursor += size;
      if (g < separators.length) out.push(separators[g]);
    }
    return out;
  }

  function buildFloor(floorNo, progress, seedText) {
    const floor = Floors.get(floorNo);
    if (!floor) throw new Error('unknown floor ' + floorNo);
    const rng = Util.createRng('floor' + floorNo + ':' + (seedText || Date.now()));
    const mk = function (t, p, role, extra) {
      return Object.assign({ templateId: t, params: p || {}, seed: rng.int(1, 1000000000), role: role, format: formatClass(t, p), kind: kindOf(t) }, extra || {});
    };
    const sig = function (item) { return item.t + ':' + JSON.stringify(item.p || {}); };

    let items;
    if (floor.fixed) {
      items = floor.own.map(function (item) { return mk(item.t, item.p, 'own', { pinned: true }); });
    } else {
      // 核（core）と 関所は 必ず出す。それ以外の枠に 抜き打ち → 持ちこし → その他 の順で入れる
      const ownGates = floor.own.filter(function (item) { return Bank.get(item.t).gate; });
      const ownFillers = floor.own.filter(function (item) { return !Bank.get(item.t).gate; });
      const cores = ownFillers.filter(function (item) { return item.core; });
      const extras = ownFillers.filter(function (item) { return !item.core; });
      const mandatory = ownGates.length + cores.length;
      // 抜き打ち（下の階の関所）: 総まとめ。9階以上は 全階から、それ以下は 近い3階から
      const HEAVY_SURPRISE = { 'f12-gate-three-ways': true };
      const lowerGates = lowerGateTemplates(floorNo).filter(function (g) { return !HEAVY_SURPRISE[g.t]; });
      const wantSurprise = floorNo >= 3 && lowerGates.length ? (floorNo >= 8 ? 2 : 1) : 0;
      // 持ちこし: その階にもともとある問題は 二重に入れない
      const ownIds = floor.own.map(function (item) { return item.t; });
      const carrySrc = ((progress && progress.carryOver) || []).filter(function (c) { return ownIds.indexOf(c.templateId) < 0; });

      // 残り枠 = count − （関所＋核）。マイナスなら 核が多い階なので count を こえる
      let leftover = Math.max(0, floor.count - mandatory);
      const carryCount = Math.min(2, carrySrc.length, leftover);   // 間違えた問題の再出題を先に
      leftover -= carryCount;
      const surpriseCount = Math.min(wantSurprise, leftover);
      leftover -= surpriseCount;
      const extraSlots = leftover;   // その他（繰り返し・仕掛け）に回せる枠

      const recent = floorNo >= 9 ? lowerGates : lowerGates.filter(function (g) { return g.floor >= floorNo - 3; });
      const pool = recent.length >= surpriseCount ? recent : lowerGates;
      const carry = carrySrc.slice(0, carryCount).map(function (c) { return mk(c.templateId, c.params, 'carry'); });
      const surprise = rng.shuffle(pool).slice(0, surpriseCount).filter(function (g) {
        return !carry.some(function (c) { return c.templateId === g.t; });
      }).map(function (g) { return mk(g.t, g.p, 'surprise', { originFloor: g.floor }); });

      // その他は「同じ問題の2回目以降」から先に落とす
      const seen = {};
      const firsts = [], repeats = [];
      extras.forEach(function (item) { const k = sig(item); if (seen[k]) repeats.push(item); else { seen[k] = true; firsts.push(item); } });
      let keep = firsts.slice();
      let repeatPool = repeats.slice();
      while (keep.length + repeatPool.length > extraSlots && repeatPool.length) repeatPool.pop();
      if (keep.length > extraSlots) keep = rng.shuffle(keep).slice(0, extraSlots);
      const chosenFillers = cores.concat(keep).concat(repeatPool);
      // 教える順を保つ
      const chosen = floor.own.filter(function (item) {
        return ownGates.indexOf(item) >= 0 || chosenFillers.indexOf(item) >= 0;
      }).map(function (item) { return mk(item.t, item.p, 'own', { pinned: Bank.get(item.t).gate, core: !!item.core }); });

      items = chosen.slice();
      carry.forEach(function (c, i) { items.splice(Math.min(items.length, 1 + i), 0, c); });
      surprise.forEach(function (s, i) {
        const pos = Math.min(items.length, Math.floor(items.length * (i + 1) / (surprise.length + 1)));
        items.splice(pos, 0, s);
      });
      items = fixFormatRuns(items);
    }

    // 同じ階の中で 同じ数字の問題が2回出ないように、かぶったら seed を引き直す
    const prompts = {};
    items.forEach(function (item) {
      for (let t = 0; t < 8; t += 1) {
        const q = Bank.build(item.templateId, item.seed, item.params);
        const pieces = q.figure && q.figure.fig ? q.figure.fig.pieces : [];
        const oneRect = pieces.length === 1 && item.templateId !== 'f3-transpose';
        const shapeKey = oneRect ? JSON.stringify([Math.min(pieces[0].w, pieces[0].h), Math.max(pieces[0].w, pieces[0].h)]) : JSON.stringify(pieces.map(function (p) { return [p.x, p.y, p.w, p.h]; }));
        const key = item.templateId + '|' + JSON.stringify(q.answer) + '|' + shapeKey;
        if (!prompts[key]) { prompts[key] = true; break; }
        item.seed = rng.int(1, 1000000000);
      }
    });

    return {
      kind: 'floor',
      floorNo: floorNo,
      count: items.length,
      items: items,
      index: 0,
      answers: [],
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  // 関所を おとしたときの みじかい 回: その階の 関所 ＋ ランダムに 2問（本人の指定 2026-09-16）
  // 階を ぜんぶ やり直すのは 重い。でも 関所の 重みは のこす
  function buildGateRedo(floorNo, progress, seedText) {
    const floor = Floors.get(floorNo);
    if (!floor) throw new Error('unknown floor ' + floorNo);
    const rng = Util.createRng('redo' + floorNo + ':' + (seedText || Date.now()));
    const mk = function (t, p, role, extra) {
      return Object.assign({ templateId: t, params: p || {}, seed: rng.int(1, 1000000000), role: role, format: formatClass(t, p), kind: kindOf(t) }, extra || {});
    };
    const isGate = function (item) { try { return !!Bank.get(item.t).gate; } catch (e) { return false; } };
    const gates = floor.own.filter(isGate);
    // 巻きぞえの 2問は、その回で まちがえた 問題を 先に えらぶ（なければ ランダム）
    const missedIds = ((progress && progress.carryOver) || []).map(function (c) { return c.templateId; });
    const pool = floor.own.filter(function (item) { return !isGate(item); });
    const missed = pool.filter(function (item) { return missedIds.indexOf(item.t) >= 0; });
    const others = missed.concat(rng.shuffle(pool.filter(function (item) { return missed.indexOf(item) < 0; }))).slice(0, 2);
    const picked = gates.concat(others);
    // 教える順は そのまま
    const items = floor.own.filter(function (item) { return picked.indexOf(item) >= 0; })
      .map(function (item) { return mk(item.t, item.p, 'own', { pinned: isGate(item), core: !!item.core }); });
    if (!items.length) return buildFloor(floorNo, progress, seedText);
    return {
      kind: 'redo',
      floorNo: floorNo,
      count: items.length,
      items: items,
      index: 0,
      answers: [],
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  function questionAt(session, index) {
    const item = session.items[index];
    const q = Bank.build(item.templateId, item.seed, item.params);
    q.role = item.role;
    q.originFloor = item.originFloor || q.floor;
    return q;
  }

  function currentQuestion(session) {
    if (session.index >= session.items.length) return null;
    return questionAt(session, session.index);
  }

  function answer(session, given, extra) {
    const q = currentQuestion(session);
    if (!q) throw new Error('session finished');
    const result = Bank.check(q, given);
    const record = {
      index: session.index,
      templateId: q.templateId,
      seed: q.seed,
      params: q.params,
      role: q.role,
      gate: q.gate,
      correct: result.correct,
      given: given,
      misconception: result.misconception || null,
      hints: (extra && extra.hints) || 0,
      fields: result.fields || null
    };
    session.answers.push(record);
    session.index += 1;
    return { result: result, record: record, done: session.index >= session.items.length };
  }

  function isDone(session) {
    return session.index >= session.items.length;
  }

  function summarize(session) {
    const wrong = session.answers.filter(function (a) { return !a.correct; });
    const gateFailures = wrong.filter(function (a) { return a.gate; });
    return {
      total: session.items.length,
      correct: session.answers.filter(function (a) { return a.correct; }).length,
      wrong: wrong,
      gateFailed: gateFailures.length > 0,
      gateFailures: gateFailures,
      surpriseFailed: gateFailures.some(function (a) { return a.role === 'surprise'; })
    };
  }

  return {
    buildFloor: buildFloor,
    buildGateRedo: buildGateRedo,
    questionAt: questionAt,
    currentQuestion: currentQuestion,
    answer: answer,
    isDone: isDone,
    summarize: summarize,
    lowerGateTemplates: lowerGateTemplates,
    fixFormatRuns: fixFormatRuns,
    kindOf: kindOf,
    maxRun: maxRun
  };
});


/* 不思議の塔: やり直し画面のロジック
 * 1. 解説（図＋一文）を見て、あなうめで正しい手順を自分でたどる（同じ問題をもう一度）
 * 2. 最後に「やり直しモード」で、元の問題に もう一度 自分で答える（デザインの やり直し画面）
 * 3. せいかいするまで出られない
 * 4. あなうめのどこで止まったかを記録。同じステップで2回止まったら、そこだけ助ける
 * 5. 3回連続で不せいかいなら、答えを見せて写させ、次の階で再出題
 */
(function (root, factory) {
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = factory(require('./util.js'), require('./bank.js'), require('./guide.js'), require('./deduce.js'), require('./hissan.js'));
  else { root.MT = root.MT || {}; root.MT.Retry = factory(root.MT.Util, root.MT.Bank, root.MT.Guide, root.MT.Deduce, root.MT.Hissan); }
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this, function (Util, Bank, Guide, Deduce, Hissan) {
  'use strict';

  const REVEAL_AFTER = 3;
  const HELP_AFTER = 2;
  // 「元の問題に もう一度 答える」ステップ。えらび直し・ならべ直し・さがし直しは それ自体が答え直し
  const REANSWER_KINDS = { choice: true, order: true, pairs: true, reanswer: true };

  function stepsOf(q) {
    const steps = ((q.explain && q.explain.steps) || []).slice();
    if (q.format === 'cut' && q.finalChoice) return steps;   // 3通りの関所は 最後の「答えは同じ?」が答え直し
    const last = steps[steps.length - 1];
    if (!last || !REANSWER_KINDS[last.kind]) steps.push({ key: 'reanswer', kind: 'reanswer', label: 'もう一度' });
    return steps;
  }

  function isReanswerStep(step) { return !!(step && REANSWER_KINDS[step.kind]); }

  function build(session) {
    const items = session.answers.filter(function (a) { return !a.correct; }).map(function (a) {
      const item = {
        index: a.index,
        templateId: a.templateId,
        seed: a.seed,
        params: a.params,
        gate: a.gate,
        given: a.given,
        stumbled: !!a.stumbled,
        misconception: a.misconception,
        fields: a.fields,
        stepIndex: 0,
        blankDone: {},         // 埋め終わった blank
        stops: {},             // ステップごとに止まった回数
        consecutiveFails: 0,
        revealed: false,
        reissue: false,
        done: false,
        helpShown: null
      };
      applyPrefill(item);
      return item;
    });
    let current = 0;
    while (current < items.length && items[current].done) current += 1;
    return { items: items, current: current, finished: current >= items.length };
  }

  function stepBlanks(step) {
    return (step.parts || []).filter(function (p) { return p && typeof p === 'object' && p.blank; });
  }

  function applyPrefill(item) {
    // 子どもが合っていたらんは チェック済みで残す（fields のとき）
    let q;
    try { q = Bank.build(item.templateId, item.seed, item.params); } catch (e) { item.done = true; return; }
    const steps = stepsOf(q);
    if (q.format === 'fields' && item.fields) {
      const okKeys = Object.keys(item.fields).filter(function (k) { return item.fields[k] && item.fields[k].correct; });
      steps.forEach(function (st) {
        if (st.kind) return;
        const blanks = stepBlanks(st);
        const owns = blanks.some(function (b) { return okKeys.indexOf(b.blank) >= 0; });
        if (owns) blanks.forEach(function (b) { item.blankDone[st.key + '.' + b.blank] = true; });
      });
    }
    item.stepIndex = skipDoneSteps(item, steps, 0);
    if (item.stepIndex >= steps.length) item.done = true;
  }

  function stepIsDone(item, step) {
    if (step.kind) return false;
    const blanks = stepBlanks(step);
    return blanks.length > 0 && blanks.every(function (b) { return item.blankDone[step.key + '.' + b.blank]; });
  }

  function skipDoneSteps(item, steps, from) {
    // 全部埋まっているあなうめステップは飛ばす（合っていたらんが後ろにあるとき）
    let idx = from;
    while (idx < steps.length && stepIsDone(item, steps[idx])) idx += 1;
    return idx;
  }

  function currentItem(state) {
    if (state.finished) return null;
    // すでに おわった 問題は とばす（保存が こわれて 番号が もどっても、ステップの ない 画面に しない）
    while (state.current < state.items.length && state.items[state.current] && state.items[state.current].done) state.current += 1;
    if (state.current >= state.items.length) { state.finished = true; return null; }
    return state.items[state.current] || null;
  }

  function questionOf(item) {
    return Bank.build(item.templateId, item.seed, item.params);
  }

  function prefill(item, q) {
    // 子どもが合っていた部分はチェック済みで残す
    const pre = {};
    if (q.format === 'fields' && item.fields) {
      Object.keys(item.fields).forEach(function (k) { if (item.fields[k].correct) pre[k] = true; });
    }
    return pre;
  }

  function view(state) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return null;
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const steps = stepsOf(q);
    const step = steps[item.stepIndex] || null;
    const pre = prefill(item, q);
    if (step && step.kind === 'cut') guideFor(state);   // 切った図を先に用意する（preset を正しく返すため）
    return {
      item: item,
      question: q,
      mode: item.misconception === 'calc' ? 'calc' : 'concept',   // 計算ミスは 式だけ短く、考え方のミスは 図＋一文＋あなうめ
      sentence: Bank.explanationFor(q, { misconception: item.misconception }),
      steps: steps,
      step: step,
      stepIndex: item.stepIndex,
      isReanswer: isReanswerStep(step),
      prefilled: pre,
      yourAnswer: item.stumbled ? '答えは合っていたけれど、とちゅうで 3回 つまずいた' : describeGiven(q, item.given),
      revealed: item.revealed,
      help: item.helpShown,
      hissan: item.hissan || null,     // 式は合っていて 計算だけまちがえたとき、筆算の途中式を1つずつ
      preset: !!(item.guide && item.guide.preset),   // 複合図形: 正しい切り方が最初から図に入っている
      remaining: state.items.length - state.current,
      redoIndex: state.current + 1,
      redoTotal: state.items.length
    };
  }

  function describeGiven(q, given) {
    if (given == null) return '';
    if (q.format === 'cut' && q.finalChoice) {
      const fc = (q.finalChoice.choices || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === given; })[0];
      return fc ? '「' + fc.text + '」' : '（答えなし）';
    }
    if (q.format === 'line') return '（線）';
    if (q.format === 'choice') {
      const c = (q.choices || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === given; })[0];
      return c ? '「' + c.text + '」' : String(given);
    }
    if (q.format === 'fields') {
      return q.fields.map(function (f) { return f.label + ' ' + (given && given[f.key] != null ? given[f.key] : '') + f.unit; }).join(' / ');
    }
    if (q.format === 'order') {
      const map = {};
      (q.order.items || []).forEach(function (i) { map[i.id] = i.text; });
      return (given || []).map(function (id) { return map[id] || id; }).join(' → ');
    }
    if (q.format === 'pairs') return (given || []).map(function (p) { return p[0] + '×' + p[1]; }).join('、');
    return String(given) + (q.unit || '');
  }

  function blanksOf(step) {
    return (step.parts || []).filter(function (p) { return p && typeof p === 'object' && p.blank; });
  }

  function nextBlank(item, step) {
    const blanks = blanksOf(step);
    for (let i = 0; i < blanks.length; i += 1) if (!item.blankDone[step.key + '.' + blanks[i].blank]) return blanks[i];
    return null;
  }

  function registerFail(state, item, step, extra) {
    item.consecutiveFails += 1;
    // 「答えを見せる」は 問題ごとに数える（とちゅうで せいかいしても 0 に もどさない。本人の決定 2026-09-15）
    item.fails = (item.fails || 0) + 1;
    if (step) item.stops[step.key] = (item.stops[step.key] || 0) + 1;
    const out = { correct: false, stops: step ? item.stops[step.key] : 0 };
    if (step && item.stops[step.key] >= HELP_AFTER && !item.helpShown) {
      item.helpShown = { step: step.key, text: helpText(step), segments: helpSegments(item, step, extra) };
      out.help = item.helpShown;
    }
    if (item.fails >= REVEAL_AFTER || item.revealed) {
      item.revealed = true;
      item.reissue = true;
      out.reveal = true;
    }
    return out;
  }

  function helpText(step) {
    const key = String(step.key || '');
    if (step.kind === 'reanswer') return 'さっき あなうめで 出した 答えを 思い出そう。';
    if (step.kind === 'choice') return '一文を もう一度読んで、図の中の言葉と 選ぶ文の言葉を 合わせてみよう。';
    if (key === 'formula' || key === 'count') return 'たては 左の辺、横は 下の辺。その 2つを かけるよ。';
    if (key === 'reverse') return '面積を、分かっている辺で わるよ。';
    if (key === 'perimeter') return 'まわりは ふちを1周した長さ。たてと横を たして 2倍。';
    if (key.indexOf('cut') === 0) {
      if (key.indexOf('-area') > 0) return 'この長方形の たて×横 だよ。';
      if (key.indexOf('sum') > 0 || key.indexOf('sub') > 0) return '分けた長方形の面積を、全部 たす（かこんだときは ひく）よ。';
      return '向かい合う辺は 同じ長さ。同じ色の2本は 同じ長さだよ。';
    }
    return '一文を もう一度読んで、図を見よう。';
  }

  function helpSegments(item, step, extra) {
    // 「この2本は同じ長さ」を図で見せるための辺の組。色は UI がつける
    let q;
    try { q = Bank.build(item.templateId, item.seed, item.params); } catch (e) { return []; }
    const spec = q.figure;
    if (!spec || !spec.fig) return [];
    const key = String(step.key || '');
    if (key === 'formula' || key === 'count' || key === 'perimeter') {
      const ys = (spec.labels || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === 'y'; }).slice(0, 1);
      const xs = (spec.labels || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === 'x'; }).slice(0, 1);
      return [ys, xs].filter(function (g) { return g.length; });
    }
    const dim = extra && extra.dim;
    if (!dim) return [];
    const hidden = (spec.hidden || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === dim.axis && l.a === dim.a && l.b === dim.b; })[0];
    if (!hidden) {
      const shown = (spec.labels || []).filter(function (l) { return l.axis === dim.axis && l.a === dim.a && l.b === dim.b; })[0];
      return shown ? [[shown]] : [];
    }
    const sol = Deduce.solve(spec.fig, spec.labels || []);
    const gap = sol.gaps[dim.axis].filter(function (g) { return g.a >= dim.a && g.b <= dim.b && g.path; })[0];
    if (!gap) return [[hidden]];
    return [[hidden].concat(gap.path.map(function (p) { return p.label; }))];
  }

  function advanceStep(state, item, q) {
    const steps = stepsOf(q);
    item.stepIndex = skipDoneSteps(item, steps, item.stepIndex + 1);
    item.consecutiveFails = 0;
    item.helpShown = null;   // 「答えを見せる」は 問題ごと。見せたら その問題の おわりまで 見せたまま（revealed は そのまま）
    if (item.stepIndex >= steps.length) {
      item.done = true;
      state.current += 1;
      if (state.current >= state.items.length) state.finished = true;
      return { correct: true, itemDone: true, finished: state.finished };
    }
    return { correct: true, stepDone: true, nextIsReanswer: isReanswerStep(steps[item.stepIndex]) };
  }

  function stepNow(item, q) {
    return stepsOf(q)[item.stepIndex];
  }

  function submitBlank(state, blankKey, value) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return { correct: false, message: 'やり直しは 終わっているよ。' };
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const step = stepNow(item, q);
    if (!step || step.kind) return { correct: false, message: 'ここは 数を入れるところではないよ。' };
    const blank = blanksOf(step).filter(function (b) { return b.blank === blankKey; })[0];
    if (!blank) return { correct: false, message: '入れるところが 見つからない。' };
    const n = Util.parseInteger(value);
    if (n == null) return { correct: false, empty: true };
    if (n !== blank.answer) {
      const fail = registerFail(state, item, step);
      // 式（この欄より前の欄）は合っていて、この欄の計算だけ ちがう → 筆算の途中式へ
      const before = blanksOf(step).slice(0, blanksOf(step).indexOf(blank));
      const formulaOk = before.length > 0 && before.every(function (b) { return item.blankDone[step.key + '.' + b.blank]; });
      if (blank.calc && formulaOk && !item.hissan) {
        item.hissan = Hissan.plan(blank.calc[0], blank.calc[1], blank.calc[2]);
        item.hissan.forBlank = blank.blank;
        item.hissan.stepKey = step.key;
        item.misconception = item.misconception || 'calc';
        fail.calc = true;
        fail.hissan = item.hissan;
      }
      return fail;
    }
    item.blankDone[step.key + '.' + blank.blank] = true;
    item.consecutiveFails = 0;
    if (nextBlank(item, step)) return { correct: true, blankDone: true };
    return advanceStep(state, item, q);
  }

  function submitHissan(state, value) {
    // 筆算の途中式を1つずつ。全部できたら、その欄は自動で埋まる
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item || !item.hissan || !Array.isArray(item.hissan.steps)) return { correct: false, message: '今は 筆算ではないよ。' };
    const h = item.hissan;
    const n = Util.parseInteger(value);
    if (n == null) return { correct: false, empty: true };   // 空は まちがいに 数えない（0 あつかいに しない）
    const res = Hissan.answer(h, n);
    if (res.empty) return { correct: false, empty: true };
    if (!res.correct) {
      const fail = registerFail(state, item, { key: 'hissan:' + (res.step ? res.step.key : '?') });
      return Object.assign(res, fail);
    }
    item.consecutiveFails = 0;
    if (!res.done) return res;
    // 全部書けた。書き終わった筆算を見せたまま、らんを埋める。次へ進むのは confirmHissan で
    item.blankDone[h.stepKey + '.' + h.forBlank] = true;
    item.hissanDone = true;
    return Object.assign(res, { hissanDone: true, showFinished: true });
  }

  function confirmHissan(state) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item || !item.hissan) return { correct: false };
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const h = item.hissan;
    const steps = stepsOf(q);
    const step = steps.filter(function (s) { return s.key === h.stepKey; })[0] || steps[item.stepIndex];
    item.hissan = null;
    item.hissanDone = false;
    if (step && nextBlank(item, step)) return { correct: true, blankDone: true };
    return advanceStep(state, item, q);
  }

  function submitChoice(state, choiceId) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return { correct: false };
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const step = stepNow(item, q);
    if (!step || step.kind !== 'choice') return { correct: false, message: '今は 選ぶところではないよ。' };
    if (choiceId == null || choiceId === '') return { correct: false, empty: true };
    const res = Bank.check(q, choiceId);
    if (!res.correct) return registerFail(state, item, step);
    return advanceStep(state, item, q);
  }

  function submitOrder(state, order) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return { correct: false };
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const step = stepNow(item, q);
    if (!step || step.kind !== 'order') return { correct: false };
    if (!Array.isArray(order) || !order.length) return { correct: false, empty: true };
    const res = Bank.check(q, order);
    if (!res.correct) return registerFail(state, item, step);
    return advanceStep(state, item, q);
  }

  function submitPairs(state, pairs) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return { correct: false };
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const step = stepNow(item, q);
    if (!step || step.kind !== 'pairs') return { correct: false };
    if (!Array.isArray(pairs) || !pairs.length) return { correct: false, empty: true };
    const res = Bank.check(q, pairs);
    if (!res.correct) return Object.assign(registerFail(state, item, step), { found: res.found, extra: res.extra });
    return advanceStep(state, item, q);
  }

  // やり直しモード: 元の問題に もう一度 答える（数・複数の欄・マス・複合図形の答え）
  function submitReanswer(state, given) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return { correct: false };
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const step = stepNow(item, q);
    if (!step || step.kind !== 'reanswer') return { correct: false, message: '今は 答え直すところではないよ。' };
    const res = Bank.check(q, given);
    if (res.empty) return { correct: false, empty: true };
    if (!res.correct) return Object.assign(registerFail(state, item, step), { fields: res.fields || null, misconception: res.misconception || null });
    return advanceStep(state, item, q);
  }

  // 複合図形（cut）のステップ: Guide を使う。ガイドは必ず full
  function guideFor(state) {
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return null;
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const step = stepNow(item, q);
    if (!step || step.kind !== 'cut') return null;
    if (!item.guide) {
      // 解説の図は「正しい見方を書き込んだ状態」。切る線は最初から引いてあり、子どもは たて・よこ・面積・たす を自分で埋める
      item.guide = Guide.create(q, step.guide === 'three' ? 'three' : 'full');
      const wanted = item.guide.mode === 'three' ? ((q.cut && q.cut.modes) || ['v', 'h', 'e']) : [null];
      Guide.presetCut(item.guide, q, wanted[0]);
    }
    return { guide: item.guide, question: q };
  }

  function guideAddLine(state, line) {
    const g = guideFor(state);
    if (!g) return { ok: false };
    return Guide.addLine(g.guide, g.question, line);
  }

  function guideAnswer(state, value) {
    const g = guideFor(state);
    if (!g) return { correct: false };
    const item = currentItem(state);
    const res = Guide.answerStep(g.guide, g.question, value);
    if (res.empty) return res;
    if (!res.correct) {
      const fail = registerFail(state, item, { key: 'cut:' + (res.step ? res.step.key : '?') }, { dim: res.step && res.step.dim });
      return Object.assign(res, fail);
    }
    item.consecutiveFails = 0;
    if (res.wayDone && res.remaining && res.remaining.length && g.guide.preset) {
      // 3通り: 次の切り方も自動で引いておく
      Guide.presetCut(g.guide, g.question, res.remaining[0]);
    }
    if (res.finished || res.allWaysDone) {
      if (res.allWaysDone && g.question.finalChoice) return Object.assign(res, { needFinalChoice: true });
      delete item.guide;
      // セッション用の finished はここでは意味がちがうので消し、やり直し用の印だけ返す
      delete res.finished;
      return Object.assign(res, { guideFinished: true }, advanceStep(state, item, g.question));
    }
    return res;
  }

  function guideFinalChoice(state, choiceId) {
    const g = guideFor(state);
    if (!g) return { correct: false };
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return { correct: false };
    if (!g.question.finalChoice || choiceId !== g.question.finalChoice.answer) return registerFail(state, item, { key: 'cut:final' });
    delete item.guide;
    return advanceStep(state, item, g.question);
  }

  function revealValue(state) {
    // 1つの問題で 3回まちがえたら、いまの ステップの 答えを見せる（写して進む）
    const item = currentItem(state);
    if (!item) return null;
    const q = questionOf(item);
    const step = stepNow(item, q);
    if (!step) return null;
    if (step.kind === 'reanswer') {
      if (q.format === 'fields') {
        const ans = {};
        q.fields.forEach(function (f) { ans[f.key] = f.answer; });
        return { kind: 'fields', answer: ans, text: q.fields.map(function (f) { return f.label + ' ' + f.answer + f.unit; }).join('、') };
      }
      return { kind: 'number', answer: q.answer, text: q.answer + (q.unit || '') };
    }
    if (step.kind === 'choice') return { kind: 'choice', answer: q.answer, text: describeGiven(q, q.answer) };
    if (step.kind === 'order') return { kind: 'order', answer: q.order.answer, text: describeGiven(q, q.order.answer) };
    if (step.kind === 'pairs') return { kind: 'pairs', answer: q.pairs.answer, text: describeGiven(q, q.pairs.answer) };
    if (item.hissan) {
      const hs = Hissan.current(item.hissan);
      return hs ? { kind: 'number', answer: hs.answer, text: hs.expr + '＝' + hs.answer } : null;
    }
    if (step.kind === 'cut') {
      const g = guideFor(state);
      if (g && g.guide.finished && g.question.finalChoice) {
        return { kind: 'choice', answer: g.question.finalChoice.answer, text: describeGiven(g.question, g.question.finalChoice.answer) };
      }
      const cs = g && Guide.currentStep(g.guide);
      return cs ? { kind: 'number', answer: cs.answer, text: cs.answer + cs.unit } : { kind: 'number', answer: q.answer, text: q.answer + (q.unit || '') };
    }
    const blank = nextBlank(item, step);
    return blank ? { kind: 'number', answer: blank.answer, text: blank.answer + blank.unit, blank: blank.blank } : null;
  }

  function summary(state) {
    const reissue = [];
    const stopStats = {};
    state.items.forEach(function (item) {
      if (item.reissue || item.gate) reissue.push({ templateId: item.templateId, params: item.params });
      Object.keys(item.stops).forEach(function (k) {
        const key = item.templateId + ':' + k;
        stopStats[key] = (stopStats[key] || 0) + item.stops[k];
      });
    });
    return { reissue: reissue, stopStats: stopStats };
  }

  return {
    build: build,
    currentItem: currentItem,
    view: view,
    stepsOf: stepsOf,
    isReanswerStep: isReanswerStep,
    submitBlank: submitBlank,
    submitHissan: submitHissan,
    confirmHissan: confirmHissan,
    submitChoice: submitChoice,
    submitOrder: submitOrder,
    submitPairs: submitPairs,
    submitReanswer: submitReanswer,
    guideFor: guideFor,
    guideAddLine: guideAddLine,
    guideAnswer: guideAnswer,
    guideFinalChoice: guideFinalChoice,
    revealValue: revealValue,
    summary: summary,
    describeGiven: describeGiven,
    REVEAL_AFTER: REVEAL_AFTER,
    HELP_AFTER: HELP_AFTER
  };
});


/* 不思議の塔: 進み具合の保存（ブラウザ内 localStorage だけ。サーバーには送らない） */
(function (root, factory) {
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = factory(require('./util.js'));
  else { root.MT = root.MT || {}; root.MT.Progress = factory(root.MT.Util); }
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this, function (Util) {
  'use strict';

  const STORAGE_KEY = 'mensekiTower.v1';
  const VERSION = 1;

  const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = { size: 'normal', kana: false, keypad: 'right' };

  function create(name) {
    return {
      version: VERSION,
      name: name || '',
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      currentFloor: 1,
      clearedFloors: [],
      roofCleared: false,
      roofSeen: false,
      howSeen: false,      // あそびかたを 見たか（はじめてのときは 大きく出す）
      floorStats: {},
      carryOver: [],
      handbook: [],
      intro: { m2Seen: false, m2Prediction: null, seen: {} },
      underground: { unlocked: false, current: 1, cleared: [], reached: 0, attempts: {} },
      session: null,
      retry: null,
      undergroundSession: null,
      lastResult: null,
      lastOutcome: null,
      stopStats: {},
      hintTotal: 0,
      sound: false,
      settings: Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_SETTINGS),
      stats: { correct: 0, answered: 0, beaten: 0, playMs: 0, lastActive: 0 },
      zukan: {},
      mode: null,          // 'rematch' のとき ずかんからの再戦中
      rematchId: null,
      stash: null          // 再戦中に よけておく ふだんの状態
    };
  }

  function num(v, d) { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? n : d; }
  // 整数だけ・範囲の中だけ・重なりなし・小さい順（こわれた保存や 写しまちがいの コード から 身をまもる）
  function uniqInts(a, lo, hi) {
    if (!Array.isArray(a)) return [];
    return a.map(Number).filter(function (n, i, arr) { return Number.isInteger(n) && n >= lo && n <= hi && arr.indexOf(n) === i; }).sort(function (x, y) { return x - y; });
  }
  function plainObj(v) { return !!v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v); }
  // 階は 1から じゅんに クリアする。とびとびの ところから 先は 数えない（[30] だけ などで エンディングが 開かないように）
  function prefixRun(sorted) {
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length && sorted[i] === i + 1; i += 1) out.push(sorted[i]);
    return out;
  }

  function normalize(raw) {
    const base = create('');
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return base;
    const p = Object.assign({}, base, raw);
    p.version = VERSION;
    p.name = String(p.name || '').slice(0, 20);
    p.currentFloor = Math.min(15, Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(p.currentFloor)) || 1));
    p.clearedFloors = prefixRun(uniqInts(p.clearedFloors, 1, 15));
    p.roofCleared = !!p.roofCleared;
    p.roofSeen = !!p.roofSeen;
    p.howSeen = !!p.howSeen;
    const fs0 = plainObj(p.floorStats) ? p.floorStats : {};
    p.floorStats = {};
    Object.keys(fs0).forEach(function (k) { if (plainObj(fs0[k])) p.floorStats[k] = fs0[k]; });
    p.carryOver = Array.isArray(p.carryOver) ? p.carryOver.filter(function (c) { return c && typeof c.templateId === 'string'; }).slice(0, 4) : [];
    p.handbook = Array.isArray(p.handbook) ? p.handbook : [];
    p.intro = Object.assign({ m2Seen: false, m2Prediction: null, seen: {} }, (p.intro && typeof p.intro === 'object') ? p.intro : {});
    if (!p.intro.seen || typeof p.intro.seen !== 'object') p.intro.seen = {};
    if (p.intro.m2Seen) p.intro.seen.m2 = true;
    p.underground = Object.assign({ unlocked: false, current: 1, cleared: [], reached: 0, attempts: {} }, (p.underground && typeof p.underground === 'object') ? p.underground : {});
    p.underground.cleared = prefixRun(uniqInts(p.underground.cleared, 1, 30));
    if (!plainObj(p.underground.attempts)) p.underground.attempts = {};
    p.underground.current = Math.min(31, Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(p.underground.current)) || 1));
    // いまの階は クリアした いちばん深い階の つぎ（地下は 1階ずつ すすむので）
    p.underground.current = p.underground.cleared.length ? Math.min(31, p.underground.cleared[p.underground.cleared.length - 1] + 1) : 1;
    p.underground.reached = Math.min(30, Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(p.underground.reached)) || 0, p.underground.cleared.length ? p.underground.cleared[p.underground.cleared.length - 1] : 0));
    // 屋上を クリアしていれば 地下は 開いている（ここが 消えると 地下に 二度と 入れない）
    p.underground.unlocked = !!p.underground.unlocked || p.roofCleared;
    p.session = normalizeSession(p.session);
    p.retry = normalizeRetry(p.retry);
    p.undergroundSession = normalizeUndergroundSession(p.undergroundSession);
    if (p.lastResult && (typeof p.lastResult !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(p.lastResult.wrong))) p.lastResult = null;
    if (p.lastResult) p.lastResult.wrong = p.lastResult.wrong.filter(function (a) { return plainObj(a) && typeof a.templateId === 'string'; });
    if (p.lastOutcome && (typeof p.lastOutcome !== 'object' || typeof p.lastOutcome.outcome !== 'string')) p.lastOutcome = null;
    p.handbook = p.handbook.filter(function (h) { return h && typeof h === 'object' && typeof h.labelId === 'string'; });
    p.stopStats = p.stopStats && typeof p.stopStats === 'object' ? p.stopStats : {};
    p.hintTotal = Number(p.hintTotal) || 0;
    p.sound = !!p.sound;
    // 設定
    const s = (p.settings && typeof p.settings === 'object') ? p.settings : {};
    p.settings = {
      size: ['small', 'normal', 'large'].indexOf(s.size) >= 0 ? s.size : DEFAULT_SETTINGS.size,
      kana: !!s.kana,
      keypad: s.keypad === 'left' ? 'left' : 'right'
    };
    // きろく
    const st = (p.stats && typeof p.stats === 'object') ? p.stats : {};
    p.stats = { correct: num(st.correct, 0), answered: num(st.answered, 0), beaten: num(st.beaten, 0), playMs: num(st.playMs, 0), lastActive: num(st.lastActive, 0) };
    const z = (p.zukan && typeof p.zukan === 'object') ? p.zukan : {};
    p.zukan = {};
    Object.keys(z).forEach(function (id) {
      const e = z[id];
      if (!e || typeof e !== 'object') return;
      p.zukan[String(id).slice(0, 30)] = { wins: num(e.wins, 0), best: num(e.best, 0), total: num(e.total, 0), plays: num(e.plays, 0) };
    });
    // 再戦
    if (p.mode === 'rematch') {
      const stash = (p.stash && typeof p.stash === 'object') ? p.stash : {};
      p.stash = { session: normalizeSession(stash.session), retry: normalizeRetry(stash.retry), lastResult: (stash.lastResult && Array.isArray(stash.lastResult.wrong)) ? stash.lastResult : null };
      p.rematchId = typeof p.rematchId === 'string' ? p.rematchId : null;
      if (!p.rematchId) { p.mode = null; }
    } else {
      p.mode = null; p.rematchId = null; p.stash = null;
    }
    return p;
  }

  function isItem(it) {
    return it && typeof it === 'object' && typeof it.templateId === 'string' && Number.isInteger(it.seed);
  }

  function normalizeSession(s) {
    if (!s || typeof s !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(s.items)) return null;
    if (!s.items.length || !s.items.every(isItem)) return null;
    const index = Number(s.index);
    if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0 || index > s.items.length) return null;
    if (!Array.isArray(s.answers) || !s.answers.every(plainObj)) return null;
    if (!Number.isInteger(s.floorNo)) return null;
    s.index = index;
    s.count = s.items.length;
    return s;
  }

  function normalizeRetry(r) {
    if (!r || typeof r !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(r.items)) return null;
    const ok = r.items.every(function (it) {
      return isItem(it) && it.stops && typeof it.stops === 'object' && it.blankDone && typeof it.blankDone === 'object' && Number.isInteger(it.stepIndex);
    });
    if (!ok) return null;
    r.current = Math.min(r.items.length, Math.max(0, Number(r.current) || 0));
    r.finished = !!r.finished || r.current >= r.items.length;
    return r;
  }

  function normalizeUndergroundSession(s) {
    if (!s || typeof s !== 'object') return null;
    if (!Number.isInteger(s.no) || s.no < 1 || s.no > 30) return null;
    if (!Number.isInteger(s.seed)) return null;
    s.stage = s.stage === 'extra' ? 'extra' : 'main';
    s.tries = Number.isInteger(s.tries) ? s.tries : 0;
    s.lines = Array.isArray(s.lines) ? s.lines.filter(function (l) { return l && typeof l === 'object'; }) : [];
    return s;
  }

  function memoryStorage() {
    const store = {};
    return {
      isMemory: true,   // 画面を とじると きえる（localStorage が使えない ブラウザの かわり）
      getItem: function (k) { return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(store, k) ? store[k] : null; },
      setItem: function (k, v) { store[k] = String(v); },
      removeItem: function (k) { delete store[k]; }
    };
  }

  function defaultStorage() {
    try {
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem('mensekiTower.probe', '1');
        localStorage.removeItem('mensekiTower.probe');
        return localStorage;
      }
    } catch (e) { /* localStorage が使えない環境 */ }
    return memoryStorage();
  }

  // info.readError: 読みこみ そのものが 失敗した（一時的な 故障）。このときは 呼んだ側が 保存を 止める
  function load(storage, info) {
    const s = storage || defaultStorage();
    let raw = null;
    try { raw = s.getItem(STORAGE_KEY); } catch (e) { if (info) info.readError = true; return null; }
    if (!raw) return null;
    try {
      return normalize(JSON.parse(raw));
    } catch (e) {
      // 壊れたデータは消さずに退避しておく（子どもの進み具合を あとで取り出せるように）
      try { s.setItem(STORAGE_KEY + '.broken', raw); } catch (e2) { /* ignore */ }
      return null;
    }
  }

  function save(storage, progress) {
    const s = storage || defaultStorage();
    progress.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    try {
      s.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(progress));
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function clear(storage) {
    const s = storage || defaultStorage();
    try { s.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
  }

  // ひきつぎコード: MT2.<中身>.<たしかめの4文字>。1文字でも 写しまちがえると はじく（MT1. は たしかめなしの 古い形）
  const CODE_PREFIX = 'MT2.';
  const OLD_PREFIX = 'MT1.';
  function checksum(text) {
    let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += 1) { h ^= text.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; }
    return ('0000' + (h % 1679616).toString(36)).slice(-4).toUpperCase();
  }

  function utf8ToBase64Url(text) {
    let binary = '';
    if (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') {
      const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
      for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 1) binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
    } else {
      binary = Buffer.from(text, 'utf8').toString('binary');
    }
    const b64 = typeof btoa === 'function' ? btoa(binary) : Buffer.from(binary, 'binary').toString('base64');
    return b64.replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/g, '');
  }

  function base64UrlToUtf8(code) {
    let clean = String(code || '').replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    while (clean.length % 4) clean += '=';
    const binary = typeof atob === 'function' ? atob(clean) : Buffer.from(clean, 'base64').toString('binary');
    if (typeof TextDecoder !== 'undefined') {
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i += 1) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
      return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
    }
    return Buffer.from(binary, 'binary').toString('utf8');
  }

  function exportCode(progress) {
    // 進み具合だけを入れる（途中の10問・やり直し・再戦の状態は入れない）
    const slim = {
      v: VERSION, name: progress.name, currentFloor: progress.currentFloor, clearedFloors: progress.clearedFloors,
      roofCleared: progress.roofCleared, roofSeen: progress.roofSeen, floorStats: progress.floorStats, carryOver: progress.carryOver, handbook: progress.handbook,
      intro: progress.intro, underground: progress.underground, hintTotal: progress.hintTotal, sound: progress.sound,
      settings: progress.settings, zukan: progress.zukan,
      stats: progress.stats ? { correct: progress.stats.correct, answered: progress.stats.answered, beaten: progress.stats.beaten, playMs: progress.stats.playMs } : undefined
    };
    const body = utf8ToBase64Url(JSON.stringify(slim));
    return CODE_PREFIX + body + '.' + checksum(body);
  }

  function importCode(code) {
    let clean = String(code || '').replace(/\s|　/g, '');
    if (clean.indexOf(CODE_PREFIX) === 0) {
      const rest = clean.slice(CODE_PREFIX.length);
      const dot = rest.lastIndexOf('.');
      if (dot < 0 || checksum(rest.slice(0, dot)) !== rest.slice(dot + 1).toUpperCase()) throw new Error('コードが ちがうみたい。1文字ずつ たしかめてね。');
      clean = rest.slice(0, dot);
    } else if (clean.indexOf(OLD_PREFIX) === 0) clean = clean.slice(OLD_PREFIX.length);
    if (!clean) throw new Error('コードを入れてね。');
    let data;
    try { data = JSON.parse(base64UrlToUtf8(clean)); } catch (e) { throw new Error('コードが ちがうみたい。もう一度 たしかめてね。'); }
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || !data.name) throw new Error('コードが ちがうみたい。もう一度 たしかめてね。');
    const p = normalize(Object.assign(create(data.name), data, { session: null, retry: null, undergroundSession: null, lastResult: null, lastOutcome: null, mode: null, stash: null, rematchId: null }));
    p.createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
    return p;
  }

  return {
    STORAGE_KEY: STORAGE_KEY,
    VERSION: VERSION,
    CODE_PREFIX: CODE_PREFIX,
    DEFAULT_SETTINGS: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
    exportCode: exportCode,
    importCode: importCode,
    create: create,
    normalize: normalize,
    load: load,
    save: save,
    clear: clear,
    memoryStorage: memoryStorage,
    defaultStorage: defaultStorage
  };
});


/* 不思議の塔: 物語の文（1画面2行まで。メタな説明や小難しい理屈は入れない） */
(function (root, factory) {
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = factory();
  else { root.MT = root.MT || {}; root.MT.Story = factory(); }
})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this, function () {
